      ;(function () {
        function applyTheme(doc) {
          try {
            var stored = localStorage.getItem('theme')
            var prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
            // 'system' (or unset) follows the OS; 'dark'/'light' are explicit choices.
            var isDark = stored === 'dark' || ((stored === 'system' || stored === null) && prefersDark)
            if (isDark) {
              doc.documentElement.classList.add('dark')
            } else {
              doc.documentElement.classList.remove('dark')
            }
          } catch (e) {}
        }

        applyTheme(document)

        // The ClientRouter copies the incoming <html> attributes over the live ones, which
        // would drop the theme class until the darkmode button re-adds it. Apply the theme
        // to the incoming document so the class survives the swap without a flash.
        document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', function (event) {
          applyTheme(event.newDocument)
        })
      })()

    </script><!-- End Google Tag Manager --><script>
      ;(function () {
        // GTM/Axeptio inject the consent banner + widget + styles into <head>/<body>
        // AFTER initial render. Astro's ClientRouter swaps <head>/<body> on
        // navigation, detaching those injected nodes: the banner disappears and
        // openAxeptioCookies() stops working (SDK DOM refs point to detached
        // nodes). A full reload re-runs GTM → re-injects Axeptio → works again.
        //
        // Fix: tag every node injected post-render, park the exact same nodes
        // under <html> (outside head/body) during the swap, then move them back
        // after. Nodes stay in the same document throughout — no cross-document
        // adoption — so the Axeptio SDK's JS references remain valid. Scripts
        // are skipped so the SDK is never re-executed (no duplicate banner).
        var PERSIST = 'data-consent-persist'
        var PARK = 'data-consent-park'
        var observer = null

        function tag(node) {
          if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) return
          if (node.tagName === 'SCRIPT') return
          if (node.hasAttribute(PERSIST) || node.hasAttribute(PARK)) return
          // Don't tag Astro's own transition/runtime nodes
          if (node.id === 'main' || node.id === 'mistral-data') return
          if (node.tagName === 'HEADER' || node.tagName === 'MAIN' || node.tagName === 'FOOTER') return
          node.setAttribute(PERSIST, '')
        }

        function observeTarget(target) {
          if (!target || !observer) return
          observer.observe(target, { childList: true })
        }

        // Start after DOM is parsed so we don't tag server-rendered head/body
        // elements still being added by the HTML parser. Axeptio is injected
        // by GTM via requestIdleCallback, which fires after DOMContentLoaded.
        function startObserver() {
          if (observer) return
          observer = new MutationObserver(function (records) {
            for (var i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
              var added = records[i].addedNodes
              for (var j = 0; j < added.length; j++) tag(added[j])
            }
          })
          observeTarget(document.head)
          observeTarget(document.body)
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
          document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', startObserver)
        } else {
          startObserver()
        }

        // Park tagged nodes under <html> so they survive the head/body swap.
        document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', function () {
          var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('[' + PERSIST + ']')
          for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
            var n = nodes[i]
            if (!n.parentNode) continue
            n.setAttribute(PARK, document.head.contains(n) ? 'head' : 'body')
            document.documentElement.appendChild(n)
          }
        })

        // Move parked nodes back into head or body.
        document.addEventListener('astro:after-swap', function () {
          var parked = document.querySelectorAll('[' + PARK + ']')
          for (var i = 0; i < parked.length; i++) {
            var n = parked[i]
            var origin = n.getAttribute(PARK)
            n.removeAttribute(PARK)
            if (origin === 'head' || n.tagName === 'STYLE' || n.tagName === 'LINK' || n.tagName === 'META') {
              document.head.appendChild(n)
            } else {
              document.body.appendChild(n)
            }
          }
          // Re-observe body in case it was replaced
          if (observer) observeTarget(document.body)
        })
      })()

</span><div class="w-full overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-start text-text-muted font-bold items-start h-7"><div class="h-auto w-full group-data-[loading='false']:-translate-y-7 transition-transform duration-300"><p class="text-body-base text-current animate-blink-slow pt-0.5 h-7">Thinking</p><p class="text-body-base text-current pt-0.5 h-7">Summary</p></div></div></div><div class="js-spoiler w-full" style="height: 0px;"><p class="text-body-base pt-4 lg:pl-10 lg:pr-5 lg:py-3 js-text opacity-0 text-text-primary">Voxtral 3 introduces a cutting-edge voice agent SDK and state-of-the-art speech models designed for real-time, multi-step conversations across 50+ languages. It combines accurate transcription, natural speech synthesis with emotional adaptability, and deep tool integration for seamless workflow automation. Available via Mistral’s EU-hosted API or self-deployment, it ensures data sovereignty while delivering low-latency, human-like interactions for contact centers, virtual assistants, and hands-free copilots.</p></div></mistral-atom-text-vibe><section class="relative w-full"><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Speech transcription and voice generation have come a long way, but building end-to-end voice agents that can listen, reason, interact, and handle complex workflows remains difficult. </p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">A reliable voice agent needs to do four things very well (and simultaneously): </p><ol class="mb-10 pl-5 flex flex-col gap-2 list-decimal"><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Listen accurately </strong>across languages, accents, speakers, and interruptions,</p></li><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Reason</strong> <strong>deeply </strong>about what users mean in specific contexts,</p></li><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Speak naturally </strong>with emotion and expression, and </p></li><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Take action </strong>over multiple steps through connected systems. </p></li></ol><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Getting all four to work together reliably in real time remains a challenge. </p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">To address this, we introduce Voxtral 3: a new family of state-of-the-art <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/studio/audio/speech-to-text/overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold"><u>speech-to-text</u></a> and <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/studio/audio/text-to-speech/overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold"><u>text-to-speech</u></a> models, alongside a voice agent SDK that brings listening, thinking, tool calling, and speaking into a single real-time streaming pipeline.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Voxtral 3 is built to create natural conversational experiences, from contact-center agents and service desks to virtual assistants and hands-free copilots. With support for dozens of languages, expressive speech, complex reasoning, and deep integration with your own tools and data, agents built with Voxtral can interpret nuanced intent, reason through sophisticated requests, and execute multi-step actions across connected systems.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">As with other Mistral models, the Voxtral 3 family is designed for sovereign deployment. Available today through Mistral&#39;s EU-hosted API or for self-deployment on your own infrastructure, it gives organizations control over where audio is processed, how data is handled, and whether conversations are retained.</p><section class="mb-10"><div class="iframe-wrapper w-full" style="aspect-ratio: 16/9"><iframe src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/xFhyfPC-VzI" style="width: 100%; height: 100%;" allowfullscreen loading="lazy" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"></iframe></div></section><h2 class="text-h3 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">Highlights</h2><ol class="mb-10 pl-5 flex flex-col gap-2 list-decimal"><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Accurate transcription across 50+ languages</strong>. The speech-to-text model, <em>Voxtral Small Transcribe 3</em>, is state-of-the-art in multilingual transcription, identifying who said what (diarization), using keyphrases to improve its accuracy (context-biasing). It detects when someone has finished speaking so agents can respond without delay (end-of-turn detection).</p></li><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Natural speech that adapts to the conversation</strong>. The text-to-speech model, <em>Voxtral Mini TTS 3</em>, introduces new voices that adjust their emotion, pace, and emphasis based on conversational context, with support for 35+ languages and 50+ dialects.</p></li><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Human-like intelligence at your (spoken) command</strong>. The new voice agent SDK combines listening, reasoning, tool calling, and speaking into a real-time experience. Agents can retrieve information, interact with business systems, and complete tasks during live conversations over phone or web.</p></li><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Pro Voice for distinctly accurate voice adaptation</strong>. For professional use cases where basic voice cloning does not suffice, Voxtral’s <em>Pro Voice</em> adaptation is able to reproduce a speaker’s distinctive character, emotional range, and speaking style across languages, using just 30 minutes of audio. </p></li><li><p class="text-body-large text-current"><strong>Your voice AI, on your infrastructure</strong>. Use Mistral&#39;s EU-hosted API or deploy Voxtral 3 on your own infrastructure. Keep sensitive conversations within your environment and control whether any audio is retained. </p></li></ol><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"><a href="https://console.mistral.ai/api-keys" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Try Voxtral now </u></a> | <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/studio/audio/overview" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Learn more</u></a></p><h2 class="text-h3 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">It hears 50+ languages, with state-of-the-art accuracy.</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"><a href="http://docs.mistral.ai/models/voxtral-small-transcribe-3-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Voxtral Transcribe 3</u></a> represents a significant milestone across transcription, diarization (who spoke when) and context biasing capabilities. The supported list of languages is expanded to 50, including 24 official EU languages and many low-resource Asian and Indic languages.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">The Small version achieves state-of-the-art results across diarization and multilingual transcription, including in English, French and Spanish. It outperforms Whisper, Scribe v2 and Gemini 3.5 Transcribe. The <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/models/voxtral-mini-transcribe-3-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Mini variant</u></a> is 4B parameters, enabling fast and accurate transcriptions at a price of $0.0035/min.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Both models support context biasing, where key terms and free-form text can be given to bias the transcription and improve recall on technical terms. This enables them to be used in production settings with domain-specific vocabulary without the need for fine-tuning.</p><mistral-block-tabs class="w-full flex flex-col gap-6 lg:gap-10"><ul class="w-full flex justify-start items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto"><li class="shrink-0"><button data-active="true" class="js-tab-trigger px-4 py-3 rounded-md border border-border-primary bg-surface-brand-secondary text-text-primary data-[active=true]:bg-surface-invert data-[active=true]:text-text-invert"><p class="text-button-large font-mistral text-current">Multilingual Context = domain-specific vocabulary and speaker names supplied to the model.</p></figcaption></figure></div></div><div class="js-tab-content data-[active=true]:block hidden w-full" data-active="false"><div class="relative flex items-center justify-center w-full object-contain"><figure class="chart flex w-full flex-col gap-5 not-prose rounded-xl border border-border-primary bg-surface-brand-secondary p-5 md:p-8 mb-10"><div class="flex flex-col gap-3"><div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-4"><div class="flex flex-col gap-1"><h3 class="text-h6 font-mistral text-text-primary" data-chart-title="true">Artificial Analysis Benchmark</h3><p class="text-body-small text-text-muted" data-chart-subtitle="true">Word Error Rate — lower is better</p></div><div class="group/chart-menu relative shrink-0" data-chart-actions data-open="false"><button type="button" class="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-border-primary text-text-muted transition-colors hover:bg-action-ghost-1 hover:text-text-primary disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 data-[open=true]:bg-action-ghost-1 data-[open=true]:text-text-primary" data-chart-menu-trigger aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Download this chart"><span class="relative inline-block w-4"><svg viewBox="0 0 30 30" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">

</span></span><audio class="js-audio" data-slug="asia-pacific-chinese-sichuan"><source src="/audio/voxtral-3-library/Chinese-Sichuan-Mei.mp3" type="audio/mpeg">Your browser does not support the audio element.</audio></button></div></div></div></mistral-block-demo-voxtral-library><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Voice Presets are available in 18 languages: Arabic (Emirati, Modern Standard), Brazilian (Portuguese), Catalan, Chinese (Mandarin), Dutch, English (American, British), French (France), German, Greek, Hindi, Italian, Korean, Norwegian, Russian, Spanish (Spain), and Vietnamese.</p><h2 class="text-h3 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">The voice agent is in control of the whole conversation.</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Voxtral 3 introduces a voice agent SDK, built on LiveKit, that runs speech in, reasoning, and speech out as one streaming pipeline. Voxtral Mini Transcribe Realtime 3 listens and detects when the caller has finished speaking, a general model—like <a href="https://docs.mistral.ai/models/mistral-small-4-0-26-03" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="underline font-semibold"><u>Mistral Small</u></a>—reasons and calls tools, and Voxtral Speech 3 replies in the tone of the conversation so far. No stage waits for the one before it, so each turn completes in under one second, and the agent stops talking when the caller cuts in.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">During a call, the agent can verify a caller&#39;s identity, check a bill, or book an appointment, using function calling, web search, and retrieval over your own systems. It answers and places calls over SIP, runs in browsers and apps over WebRTC, and connects to the telephony, IVR, and contact-center software you already use.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10"></p><figure class="mb-10"><img src="/_astro/diagram-voxtral-alt_Z2ufcLf.webp?dpl=6ad7d2609d879d2481cef4a7" /_astro/diagram-voxtral-alt_iick9.webp?dpl=6ad7d2609d879d2481cef4a7 1280w, /_astro/diagram-voxtral-alt_1j0WYp.webp?dpl=6ad7d2609d879d2481cef4a7 1920w" alt sizes="(max-width: 640px) 400px, (max-width: 1024px) 768px, (max-width: 1440px) 1280px, 1920px" loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="2400" height="1601" class="w-full"></figure><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Each stage is a separate model, so you can change the LLM, the voice, or the language without rewriting the agent, and run the whole stack on Mistral&#39;s API in Europe or on your own infrastructure.</p><h2 class="text-h3 font-mistral text-current mb-5 pb-5 border-b border-border-primary not-first:mt-16">Get started.</h2><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">Voxtral 3 is available today through the Mistral API, hosted through our European, US, and global endpoints.</p><p class="text-body-large text-text-primary mb-10">You can test out voice agents, audio transcription, speech
